We prove a localization budget, valid at boundary points: models with Lipschitz constant at most L differing by eta at a point disagree above tolerance eps on a region of volume at least kappa((eta-eps)/L)^(d+m); the discontinuous reset modes studied pay no such budget. With real LLM synthesis, GPT-5.x repairs an omitted 1D clamp in 105 of 111 mode-containing draws -- every attempt exact on 50 of 56 instrument-stream blocks (95% CI [0.781, 0.960]). On 2D regions no artifact recovers the rule (0/156); eight targeted interventions leave the failure in place, and positive controls locate it: a located rule is not induced, while given form and location the constants follow exactly. A version-space certificate proves identification is class-relative: at the widest dose the declared fit succeeds in 20/20 blocks and every sample-consistent circle is within tolerance in 18/20. We prove a class of entry rules exactly consistent with every sample yet harmless at play, so identifiability is a measurable property of the instrument. Re-scoring all 1034 artifacts on independent samples confirms acceptance certifies sample consistency and no more: where the gate is provably informative it covers about two percent of the exploited planner's queries.

</details>


### [71] [Understanding the Surprising Generalization Properties of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17957)
*Nour Shaheen,Junwei Ma,Alex Labach,Frank Hutter,Valentin Thomas,Anthony L. Caterini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现仅通过单一真实表格的自监督预训练就能涌现出惊人的跨表迁移能力，并进一步提出任务中心视角：预训练的关键在于任务数量与质量，而非数据规模。研究还表明表格的泛化本质上是基于检索的，并提供了细粒度列级预处理优于数据集级过滤的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFMs）通常依赖大规模合成语料或海量真实数据集进行预训练，但其内在机制尚不清晰。作者希望探究：仅从单一真实表格能否涌现出强迁移能力？哪些因素决定预训练效果？以及TFMs泛化的底层机制是什么？

Method: 作者在单一真实表格上执行自监督预训练，系统分析预训练效果与下游任务表现的关系。通过对比不同表格、不同特征数和实例数的表现，发现表格的"有用性"主要由特征数而非实例数决定。此外，在语料构建方面，作者比较了列级预处理与数据集级过滤/去重对下游性能的影响；最后提出基于检索的泛化视角来解释TFMs的行为。

Result: （1）单一真实表格的自监督预训练即可产生强迁移效果；（2）表格要么广泛有用要么广泛无用，与下游任务类型无关；特征数是预训练效果的最强预测因子；（3）列级细粒度预处理一致地提升下游性能，而数据集级过滤和去重没有带来改善。

Conclusion: 作者提出任务中心视角：TFMs预训练的本质是学习大量高质量任务，而非依赖数据规模。并进一步提出检索式泛化框架——好的模型应能识别上下文中的相关示例并有效聚合信息，为未来模型架构和语料设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) increasingly rely on in-context learning, where a model receives labelled examples at inference time and predicts labels for new inputs without updating its weights. Existing TFMs are typically trained on either massive synthetic corpora or very large collections of real datasets. In contrast, we show that surprisingly strong transfer can emerge from self-supervised pre-training on just a single real table. In this setting, we also find that tables tend to be either broadly useful or broadly poor regardless of downstream prediction task, and that the strongest predictor of usefulness is the number of features rather than the number of instances. This leads to a task-centric interpretation of tabular pre-training: the number and the quality of tasks are essential for the pre-training of TFMs.
  We show that the same task-centric perspective can help corpus design at scale: fine-grained column-level pre-processing consistently improves downstream performance, while no improvements are observed when we filter or deduplicate at the dataset level.
  Finally, we offer a new perspective for how TFMs generalize: we believe that tabular in-context generalization is largely retrieval-based, and good models are those that learn to identify relevant examples in the provided context and aggregate them well. The mechanics of TFMs have been relatively understudied; our task-centric, retrieval-based perspective offers a new framework to guide future model and corpus design.

</details>


### [72] [Too Sure to Be Safe: Model Calibration for Reliable Log Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17965)
*Bin Li,Dongdong Wang,Siyang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LoRD，一种轻量级的post-hoc校准框架，用于解决日志异常检测中语言模型过度自信但置信度校准不良的问题，通过预测路由相关的重构距离来估计可靠性并选择性重校准高风险预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语言模型的日志异常检测器虽性能优异，但其置信度估计校准不良，尤其在类别不平衡下对异常日志的预测过度自信。更关键的是，即使传统校准指标良好，错误预测的置信度仍持续偏高，这对运维监控系统的可靠性构成严重威胁。

Method: LoRD框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 从正确分类的验证样本的潜在表示中学习预测路由相关的可靠性模型，通过路由级重构距离估计预测可靠性；(2) 基于估计的可靠性，选择性地对高风险预测进行重校准，抑制过度自信的错误预测，同时保留可靠预测。

Result: 在四个大规模日志基准数据集和多种语言模型检测器上的实验表明，LoRD持续提升置信度可靠性，大幅减少与异常相关的过度自信错误，且不牺牲异常检测性能。

Conclusion: LoRD提供了一种有效且轻量的post-hoc解决方案，填补了日志异常检测中置信度校准与实际可靠性之间的鸿沟，为运维监控场景提供了更可靠的异常检测保障。

Abstract: Online log anomaly detection is critical for maintaining the reliability of large-scale computing systems. Although recent language model-based log anomaly detectors achieve strong detection performance, their confidence estimates remain poorly calibrated. We show that these detectors frequently assign excessive confidence to incorrect predictions, particularly for anomalous logs under severe class imbalance. Moreover, confidence on erroneous predictions remains persistently high even when conventional calibration metrics indicate good calibration, creating a critical reliability gap for operational monitoring systems. To address this issue, we propose Log Reconstruction and Distance (LoRD), a lightweight post-hoc calibration framework for reliable log anomaly detection. LoRD learns prediction-route-specific reliability models from latent representations of correctly classified validation samples and estimates prediction reliability through route-wise reconstruction distances. Based on the estimated reliability, LoRD selectively recalibrates high-risk predictions to suppress overconfident errors while preserving reliable predictions. Extensive experiments on four large-scale log benchmark datasets and multiple language model-based detectors demonstrate that LoRD consistently improves confidence reliability and substantially reduces overconfident anomaly-related errors without sacrificing anomaly detection performance.

</details>


### [73] [Evaluating and improving crop-yield forecasting methods during extreme drought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17971)
*Shrey Gupta,Yi Ming,George Mohler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了极端干旱年（2012年美国中西部玉米带）县级玉米产量预测问题，比较了传统机器学习（非深度学习）与深度学习模型在该场景下的表现，并提出了样本加权和特征选择两种改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化对粮食生产影响巨大，极端事件（如2012年玉米带干旱）考验现有作物预测模型的极限。训练与测试数据特征分布存在差异（极端干旱条件超出历史观测范围），且数据存在时空稀疏性，需要探索如何改进模型以应对此类极端场景。

Method: 使用16个气象驱动因子作为预测变量，比较ML（非深度学习）与深度学习模型预测2012年极端干旱年份县级玉米产量。针对训练/测试特征分布差异及时空稀疏性问题，引入样本加权和特征选择作为模型改进方案。

Result: 样本加权和特征选择显著改善了传统ML模型的预测性能；但深度学习模型VITA改进甚微。无论是否经过修改，VITA均优于ML模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明训练与测试特征分布差异对预测模型有重要影响；深度学习模型在应对极端事件时具有更强的鲁棒性；提出的改进方法对非深度学习模型有效，但对深度学习模型效果有限。

Abstract: The impact of climate variability on food production has led to the creation of various forecasting models that uses machine learning (ML), numerical weather predictors (NWP) or a hybrid of ML-NWP models to identify structural and physical relationships between meteorological drivers and crop growth, in order to predict crop yield. Droughts, for example the 2012 Midwestern US (Corn Belt) drought, are extreme events that affect crop production and test the limits of these forecasting models. Using 16 meteorological drivers as predictors, we compare ML (non-deep learning) and deep learning forecasting models to predict the county-level corn yield for the extreme drought year, 2012. This forecasting problem is characterized by a dissimilarity between the feature distributions of the training and test data, where the meteorological conditions of the extreme drought year fall outside the range of historically observed values. Additionally, the dataset consists of spatial and temporal irregularities where counties with missing yields introduce spatial sparsity and the use of only a subset of daily values per year introduce temporal sparsity. To overcome this, we use sample weighting and feature selection as modifications to improve our forecasting models. These modifications lead to an improvement for ML models; however, the deep learning model VITA shows little to no improvement. While VITA outperforms the ML models with or without modifications, our current study sheds light on the effect of dissimilarity between train and test feature distributions on forecasting models, compares deep learning versus non-deep learning models, and introduces modifications that are effective for non-deep learning models.

</details>


### [74] [Recirculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17981)
*Michael C. Mozer,Shoaib Ahmed Siddiqui,Danny Sawyer,Sunny Sanyal,Rosanne Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种推理时架构增强技术"再循环"（recirculation），通过在预填充阶段引入特定形式的递归，使现成的预训练基础模型能够作为动态系统跟踪信念状态，在不增加生成延迟的前提下显著降低困惑度并提升推理任务准确性，且无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈Transformer中的状态更新受限于模型深度，模型无法在推理过程中显式跟踪信念状态，这限制了其在生成和推理任务上的性能。现有方案如思维链（CoT）、循环深度技术（looping）和循环Transformer训练都存在各自局限：CoT更适合复杂推理而非基础状态跟踪，looping技术有限，循环Transformer训练成本高昂。

Method: 提出"再循环"（recirculation）技术：在预填充阶段引入一种特定形式的递归，使模型作为动态系统运行并跟踪信念状态。与CoT和深度循环技术明确区分。同时提出自适应变体，仅需轻量超参数调优即可，冻结原始模型权重，利用模型自身特性来指导架构修改而非人为强制设计。

Result: 在Gemma3模型家族上，自适应再循环相比现成基线实现了：困惑度降低23%（多数据集），GSM8k准确率提升21%，其他下游任务也有稳定提升。该方法为训练无关（training-free），在生成阶段几乎零额外延迟。

Conclusion: 该训练无关的方法通过利用模型自身来指导架构修改，证明了可以基于已训练网络的特性进行架构演化，而非依赖人为任意的设计选择，为架构改进提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We describe an inference-time architectural enhancement for off-the-shelf foundation models that markedly reduces perplexity and boosts accuracy across generation and reasoning tasks. Our approach incurs essentially no additional latency during generation, though it requires serial processing in the prefill phase. Motivated by the fundamental limitation that state updates in feedforward transformers are bounded by model depth, our technique, recirculation, introduces a specific form of recurrence that allows the model to act as a dynamical system and track belief states. We distinguish this technique from chain-of-thought computation---which is better reserved for complex inferences rather than basic state tracking---as well as from popular depth-recurrence techniques (looping) and the costly training of recurrent transformers. We also propose and evaluate an adaptive variant of recirculation which requires only light tuning of hyperparameters while freezing the original model weights. Relative to the off-the-shelf baseline, adaptive recirculation achieves remarkable gains on the Gemma3 family, including a 23% reduction in perplexity on a suite of datasets, a 21% increase in accuracy on GSM8k, and reliable improvements in accuracy on other downstream tasks. Our training-free approach succeeds by leveraging the model itself to inform architectural modifications, suggesting a route to architectural evolution guided by a trained network's properties rather than forced, arbitrary design choices.

</details>


### [75] [Composing Flow-Matching Energies with Known Physics: Generation, OOD Detection, and Inversion on PDE Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18004)
*Yixuan Sun,Anirban Samaddar,Sandeep Madireddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将流匹配模型与能量模型相结合的方法，通过势诱导速度获得显式标量能量函数，该能量函数可同时用于能量校正数据生成、OOD检测和逆问题的组合后验采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理场建模既需要数据驱动先验，也需要已知物理结构（如控制方程）。能量模型（EBMs）由于能量可加性，天然适合在推理时注入物理信息，但受限于难以处理的配分函数，训练和采样都很困难。本文旨在克服EBM的局限性，同时保留其能量可组合的优势。

Method: 本文证明带有势诱导速度的流匹配模型在所有传输时间上都能产生显式标量能量，其梯度恰好是转换后的学习得分，在总体最优时恢复边际负对数密度。时间相关能量函数仅从独立线性高斯插值上的匹配回归目标获得，无需变分形式或额外MCMC步骤，采样仍保留流ODE。这一显式能量可应用于三个场景：能量校正的数据生成、作为OOD检测的评分函数、以及能量组合后验采样用于逆问题。

Result: 显式能量允许在预测-校正采样框架中使用通用MCMC采样器，相比流ODE基线降低了PDE残差和谱距离。在OOD任务中，利用数据能量和基于物理的能量（如PDE残差）作为互补机制提高了检测精度。此外，通过与二次观测似然组合得到后验能量，探索了逆问题中基于MCMC的推理。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了EBM难以训练和采样的问题，通过流匹配模型获得显式可用的能量函数，既保留了物理信息的可加性注入能力，又实现了更优的生成质量、OOD检测性能和逆问题推理效果。

Abstract: Probabilistic modeling of physical fields benefits from both a data-driven prior and known physical structure such as the governing equations. Energy-based models (EBMs) are a natural fit since energies compose additively, which enables augmenting physics information during inference. However, EBMs have been difficult to train and sample from due to the intractable partition function. We show in this work that flow matching models with a potential-induced velocity yield an explicit scalar energy at all transport times, whose gradient is exactly the converted learned score and which recovers the marginal negative log-density at the population optimum. The time-dependent energy functions are obtained purely from the matching regression objective on an independent linear Gaussian interpolation, without a variational form or additional MCMC steps, and the sampling retains the flow ODE. Access to the energy function from a trained model serves three roles: energy-corrected data generation, energy as a scoring function for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, and energy compositional posterior sampling for inverse problems. In particular, we show the explicit energy permits general MCMC samplers in the predictor-corrector sampling framework, reducing PDE residual and spectral distance compared to the flow ODE baseline. Furthermore, we demonstrate utilizing the data energy and physics-based energy (e.g., PDE residuals) as complementary mechanisms to improve detection accuracy for OOD tasks. In addition, we explore the connection to MCMC-based inference for inverse problems by composing the energy with a quadratic observational likelihood that yields a posterior energy, used as an explicitly chosen family of inference-time targets.

</details>


### [76] [Policy-Invariant Reward Shaping from LLM Feedback: A Framework for Hybrid RL Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18008)
*Christophe D. Hounwanou,John Emeka Eze,Yaé U. Gaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了"LLM规划器+RL控制器"混合架构为Goal-Augmented MDP，证明当LLM逐状态进度分数作为有界势函数时，即使分数不准确，其产生的塑形项仍能保持最优策略集合不变，并通过数值实验（含放大20倍的对抗配置）验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM与强化学习结合日益普遍，但LLM衍生的奖励信号的理论地位常常未被明确阐述。作者希望厘清LLM-as-reward方法的理论保证，并探索比通用LLM奖励方法更强的最优性保障。

Method: 将混合架构形式化为Goal-Augmented MDP；证明当LLM逐状态进度分数满足有界势函数条件时，相应的势能塑形项保持最优策略集不变（即使LLM分数不准确）；在小型MDP上通过四种势配置（含放大至基础奖励20倍的对抗性配置）进行数值验证。

Result: 理论上证明了LLM进度分数作为有界势函数时，塑形项不改变最优策略集，该保证比一般LLM-as-reward方法更强；数值实验在四种势配置下（含对抗性显著放大配置）均验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: LLM提供的进度信号若被构造为有界势函数，则可作为安全的奖励塑形项，在LLM打分不准确甚至被对抗性放大的情况下，仍然保持最优策略不变，为LLM引导的RL提供了理论基础保证。

Abstract: Combining large language models with reinforcement learning is increasingly explored, yet the theoretical status of LLM-derived reward signals is often left implicit. We formalize the hybrid LLM-planner and RL-controller architecture as a Goal-Augmented Markov Decision Process and show that when the LLM per-state progress score is used as a bounded potential function, the resulting shaping term preserves the optimal policy set even when the LLM scores are inaccurate. This guarantee is stronger than what general LLM-as-reward approaches provide. We verify the result numerically on a small MDP under four potential configurations, including an adversarial one scaled to twenty times the base reward magnitude.

</details>


### [77] [Revisiting WEASEL 2.0: Reproduction, Sensitivity, and an Adaptive Ensemble-Size Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18021)
*Cian Higgins,Gerard Carrigan,Pinar Sungu Isiacik,Georgiana Ifrim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现了WEASEL 2.0在114个UCR数据集上的性能（平均准确率0.865，中位数0.928，与原论文无显著差异，Wilcoxon符号秩检验p=0.655），并通过扰动测试检验四个设计选择的敏感性，发现最大集成规模规则对长序列数据集过度配置，据此提出基于序列长度和类别数的自适应规则，在几乎不损失准确率的前提下显著降低内存和拟合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WEASEL 2.0作为基于字典的时间序列分类器，其最大集成规模和最大窗口大小两个超参数由简单阈值规则指定，但原论文未对这些阈值的选取进行经验验证，因此需要系统性地复现并检验这些设计选择的敏感性与合理性。

Method: 作者在114个UCR数据集上复现WEASEL 2.0，验证其与原论文结果的一致性；随后对四个设计选择（下游分类器、特征加权缺失、最大窗口规则、最大集成规模规则）进行敏感性测试；最后针对过度配置的问题，提出一个根据序列长度和类别数自适应设定最大集成规模的规则，并在固定长度数据集上评估其效果。

Result: 前三个设计选择对扰动具有鲁棒性；最大集成规模规则对长序列数据集过度配置。提出的自适应规则在固定长度数据集上，峰值拟合内存中位数减少37 MB（均值395 MB），拟合时间中位数减少0.4秒（均值4秒），准确率中位数无变化（均值仅-0.11%）。内存和时间节省集中在长序列数据集上。

Conclusion: WEASEL 2.0的复现结果与原论文高度一致，其大部分设计选择（下游分类器、特征加权、最大窗口规则）是稳健的，但最大集成规模规则需要改进。提出的自适应规则能在保持分类性能的同时显著减少计算资源消耗，改进方向具有实用价值。

Abstract: WEASEL 2.0 is a dictionary-based time series classifier that combines dilated sliding windows with a randomised hyperparameter ensemble and a fixed-size dense feature representation. Two of its hyperparameter choices, the maximum ensemble size and the maximum window size, are specified by simple thresholding rules whose chosen thresholds are not empirically justified in the original paper. In this work we reproduce WEASEL 2.0 on 114 UCR datasets, achieving a mean accuracy of 0.865 and median of 0.928, closely matching the published values (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p = 0.655). We then test the sensitivity of four design choices: the downstream classifier, the absence of feature weighting, the maximum window-size rule, and the maximum ensemble-size rule. The first three are robust to perturbation. The fourth is over-provisioned for long-series datasets, motivating an adaptive rule that sets the maximum ensemble size from series length and number of classes. Evaluated on fixed-length datasets, the adaptive rule reduces peak fit memory by a median of 37 MB (mean 395 MB) and fit time by a median of 0.4 s (mean 4 s), with a median accuracy change of 0% (mean -0.11%). Memory and time savings concentrate on long-series datasets where the original rule allocates the largest ensemble size.

</details>


### [78] [Why GPT-Style Models Do Not Directly Transfer to Symbolic Music: Compression in the Wrong Coordinate System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18025)
*Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"效果-无损性框架"（Effectiveness-Losslessness Framework），重新定义符号音乐分词的目标：不是寻找更大的音乐组合单元，而是构建在预测上有效且关系上无损的坐标系。分词应在"事实-分词边界"（解耦与去嵌套）与"分词-状态边界"（停止在固定关系之前）之间取得平衡，确保预测可压缩性与上下文关系自由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT模型在语言上成功的关键来自词元化带来的压缩效应，而非可复用组合本身。符号音乐的分词不能简单类比语言词元。关键问题在于找到使音乐规律形成稳定条件分布的坐标系，而非构造更大的音乐组合。作者发现现有方法忽视了分词的本质在于预测性压缩，而非单元大小。

Method: 提出两个基本原则：(1) 预测有效性原则（Predictive Effectiveness Principle）定义"事实-分词边界"，通过解耦（decoupling）和去嵌套（denesting）构造暴露预测规律的坐标接口；(2) 关系无损性原则（Relational Losslessness Principle）定义"分词-状态边界"，在固定上下文相关关系之前停止分词，将关系计算留给模型状态。通过受控符号音乐实验验证这些边界。

Result: 实验结果表明：有效的坐标构造能提高预测可压缩性；固定关系投影会限制上下文建模；序列压缩本身不能保证预测性压缩；保留上下文自由可以让高阶音乐组织在没有显式结构标签的情况下涌现。证实GPT风格架构可以跨模态迁移，但分词接口不能直接迁移。

Conclusion: 分词必须发现有效的表征，同时保留关系自由，使上下文结构能够涌现。GPT风格架构可以跨模态迁移，但分词接口需要针对每种模态重新设计，因为不同模态的预测规律和关系结构不同。

Abstract: GPT-style models achieve strong performance by representing language with finite vocabularies of reusable discrete tokens. This success has motivated symbolic music tokenizations to treat recurring musical structures, such as chords, motifs, and phrases, as reusable units analogous to linguistic tokens. However, tokenization derives its advantage not from reusable combinations alone, but from compression: effective compression requires coordinates in which recurring regularities form stable and predictable conditional distributions. The key problem is therefore not to find larger musical combinations, but to discover the coordinate system in which musical facts become predictively compressible. We formulate the Effectiveness--Losslessness Framework and define tokenization as the construction of a predictively effective and relationally lossless coordinate system. The Predictive Effectiveness Principle defines the Fact--Token Boundary: decoupling and denesting construct coordinate interfaces that expose predictive regularities. The Relational Losslessness Principle defines the Token--State Boundary: tokenization stops before context-dependent relations are fixed, leaving their computation to model states. Controlled symbolic-music experiments validate these boundaries. Effective coordinate construction improves predictive compressibility, while fixed relational projections constrain contextual modeling. Sequence compaction alone does not guarantee predictive compression, while preserving contextual freedom allows higher-order musical organization to emerge without explicit structural labels. These results reveal why GPT-style models do not transfer directly across modalities: architectures transfer, but tokenization interfaces do not. Tokenization must discover effective representations while preserving the relational freedom from which contextual structure can emerge.

</details>


### [79] [TabNSM: Neural Sparse Mixer for Tabular Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18026)
*Ali Eslamian,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TabNSM，一个可扩展的深度表格回归框架，结合稀疏注意力与混合器架构，通过自适应稀疏交互模块（ASIM）实现近线性复杂度，并引入多阶段回归头、GridLoss（基于序数的软分箱损失）和RISE（基于损失分位数的困难感知采样）三个组件，在9个真实回归基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型高维表格回归问题具有挑战性：树模型虽稳健但缺乏端到端表示学习能力，而深度模型虽支持灵活的特征学习但交互建模成本高且对噪声/冗余特征敏感。现有方法难以兼顾表示学习能力、计算效率和对高维异构数据的鲁棒性。

Method: TabNSM框架包含：1) 核心模块ASIM，集成前景特征发现、稀疏局部交互编码和特征-令牌混合，在固定稀疏配置下实现近线性复杂度；2) 多阶段回归头，进行渐进式预测精化；3) GridLoss损失函数，基于序感知的软分箱方式将目标结构融入表示学习；4) RISE采样策略，基于损失分位数分箱实现困难感知的样本重加权。

Result: 在九个真实回归基准上，TabNSM展现出强大的预测性能和实用可扩展性，在高维且异构的数据集上取得了尤为一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 选择性交互建模、结构化回归监督和困难感知采样相结合，为深度表格回归提供了一种有效且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Large-scale, high-dimensional tabular regression remains challenging: tree-based models are robust but lack end-to-end representation learning, while deep models enable flexible feature learning but often incur costly interaction modeling and sensitivity to noisy or redundant features. We propose TabNSM, a scalable regression framework that extends our earlier sparse-attention and mixer architectures. At its core, the Adaptive Sparse Interaction Module (ASIM) integrates foreground feature discovery, sparse local interaction encoding, and Feature-Token Mixing, providing near-linear complexity under fixed sparse configurations. For regression, TabNSM introduces three complementary components: a Multi-Stage Regression Head for progressive prediction refinement; GridLoss, an ordinal-aware soft-binning objective that incorporates target structure into representation learning; and RISE (Reweighted Instance Sampling by Error), a difficulty-aware sampling strategy based on loss-quantile bins. Across nine real-world regression benchmarks, TabNSM delivers strong predictive performance and practical scalability, with particularly consistent gains on high-dimensional and heterogeneous datasets. These results demonstrate that selective interaction modeling, structured regression supervision, and difficulty-aware sampling provide an effective and scalable approach to deep tabular regression.

</details>


### [80] [Optimize Your Sampling: Tuned Diffusion Sampling with Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18040)
*Travis Zhang,Christian Belardi,Justin Lovelace,Jin Peng Zhou,Saebyeol Shin,Carla P. Gomes,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OYS方法，将扩散模型的采样时间步选择视为黑盒优化问题，直接使用贝叶斯优化优化目标指标（如图像质量），在文本到图像生成、图像修复等任务上优于默认调度和Align Your Steps方法，无需额外训练，5步调度可保留50步调度89%-94%的质量且推理成本降低10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型采样通常需要多次前向传播通过大型神经网络，生成计算成本高。已有工作多关注高效求解器和采样器，但对采样时间步本身的选择关注较少。现有工作优化理论导出的代理指标而非真实质量指标，缺乏直接优化目标质量的方法。

Method: 提出Optimizing Your Sampling (OYS)，将时间步选择视为黑盒优化问题，采用贝叶斯优化直接优化目标指标（而非代理指标），无需额外训练，适用于包括蒸馏模型在内的各种模型，可改进简单和复杂采样器（如Euler和DPM-Solver++）。

Result: OYS在文本到图像生成任务上优于默认调度和Align Your Steps，在图像修复等任务上优于默认调度，定量和人工评估均表现优异。5步OYS调度可保留50步调度89%-94%的质量，同时将推理成本降低10倍。

Conclusion: OYS证明直接优化采样质量指标比优化代理指标更有效，作为一种通用的时间步选择优化框架，无需训练即可显著提升扩散模型采样的效率与质量，尤其在小步数（如5步）场景下效果突出。

Abstract: Sampling from a diffusion model typically requires many forward passes through a large neural network, making generation computationally expensive. While much work has focused on efficient solvers and samplers, comparatively little attention has been paid to selecting the sampling timesteps themselves. A recent line of work optimizes theoretically derived surrogates for sample quality rather than the quality metric itself. We propose Optimizing Your Sampling (OYS), which instead treats timestep selection as a black-box optimization problem, optimizing the target metric directly with Bayesian optimization. OYS outperforms both the default schedules and those of Align Your Steps on text-to-image generation, and improves over the default schedules on inpainting and other image tasks, in both quantitative and human evaluations. OYS requires no additional training, is applicable even to distilled models, and improves both simple and sophisticated samplers such as Euler and DPM-Solver++. A 5-step OYS schedule retains 89%-94% of the quality of a 50-step schedule while reducing inference cost by 10x.

</details>


### [81] [The concentration game: Bayesian updating, regret, and information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18061)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个学习者与自然之间的二人零和重复博弈，其值恒等式能同时生成贝叶斯更新和指数权重遗憾的精确核算，并为广泛的集中现象提供比较器类的变分形式。终局收益是比较器在固定相对熵下相对先验的最大收益，单步约束是自然在学习者混合行动下的信息预算。吉布斯/贝叶斯权重被证明是唯一的贝尔曼均衡器（使每轮损失独立于自然移动方向），对数配分函数作为值函数。遗憾被精确分解为三个部分：每轮信息损失、加性重调温漂移（精确核算轮次间度量尺度变化）、以及比较器相对先验携带的信息。方差和有界范围代理是此分解的宽松松弛，该分解具有一般性并支配所有标准遗憾界。两种博弈者的策略均可逐项从分解中读出，重复博弈产生自博弈的信息论账本。该比较器类几何还解释了经典大偏差界，赌徒机、后验采样、聚合和提升方法均为同一遗憾分解的特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗憾界通常依赖方差或有界范围等代理量来松弛化分析，缺乏一个统一的、精确的信息论框架来解释指数权重（吉布斯/贝叶斯）算法的遗憾来源，也无法统一解释大偏差现象与各类在线学习方法的共同几何结构。

Method: 作者构造了一个二人零和重复博弈：学习者的混合行动完全不受限，但自然的每次移动受信息预算约束（相对熵约束）；终局收益定义为比较器在固定相对熵下相对先验的最大收益。通过贝尔曼方程求解，证明吉布斯/贝叶斯权重是唯一的贝尔曼均衡器，对数配分函数充当值函数，从而得到遗憾的精确三项分解。

Result: 遗憾精确分解为：每轮信息损失（反映观测结果的变异性）、加性重调温漂移（精确核算轮次间度量尺度变化）、比较器相对先验携带的信息。该分解具有一般性，方差和有界范围代理是它的宽松版本。两种博弈者的策略可直接从分解中逐项读出，重复博弈形成自博弈的信息论账本。经典大偏差界以及赌徒机、后验采样、聚合、提升等方法均可由该分解统一导出。

Conclusion: 该博弈框架提供了一个统一的、精确的信息论视角来理解和核算指数权重/贝叶斯算法的遗憾，替代了传统二次变差代理，并统一了广泛的集中现象与在线学习方法。

Abstract: We give a two-player zero-sum repeated game between a learner and nature whose value identity generates Bayesian updating and an exact accounting of exponential-weights regret at once, and supplies the comparator-class variational form that a wide class of concentration phenomena share. The terminal payoff is the most a comparator can gain at fixed relative entropy from the prior, and the one-step constraint is an information budget on nature's move under the learner's mixed action. With the learner's move otherwise unrestricted, Gibbs/Bayes weights emerge as its unique Bellman equalizer -- the mixed action that makes the per-round loss independent of which direction nature moves -- with log-partition functions playing the role of value functions. The regret decomposes exactly into three parts: a per-round information loss reflecting the variation in observed outcomes, an additive retempering drift that accounts exactly for any change of measurement scale between rounds, and the information the comparator carries relative to the prior. The variance and bounded-range proxies that drive standard regret bounds are looser relaxations of this decomposition, which holds generally and governs them all. Both players' strategies are read off from the decomposition term by term, and repeated play yields an information-theoretic ledger of self-play in place of the usual quadratic-variation surrogate. The same comparator-class geometry accounts for the classical large-deviation bounds, and methods across bandits, posterior sampling, aggregation, and boosting are specializations of the one regret decomposition.

</details>

### [82] [Brief Announcement: Fair Binding for Hidden-State Authorization in Byzantine SMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17349)
*Arnab Mallick*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究拜占庭状态机复制（Byzantine SMR）中隐藏可消耗资源的分配问题。在代理授权场景下，命令的有效性依赖于提交的策略状态，而验证者无法从日志中重建该状态。论文识别出安全实时分配的两个独立要求：到达顺序约束提交顺序（fair-ordering），以及已提交的首个请求必须绑定后续有效性，使冲突的后续请求失效。通过显式授权见证接口，给出一个公平预留/使用协议，同时满足授权安全性和首到达活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的拜占庭SMR假设验证者可以评估有序命令的有效性，但在代理授权（agent authorization）模式下，命令的有效性仅相对于隐藏的、无法从日志中前缀重建的策略状态而言。这导致旧授权证明只是历史性认证，无法保留隐藏资源供后续使用。需要解决在拜占庭领导下安全地实时分配隐藏可消耗资源的问题。

Method: 作者隔离出两个独立要求：(1) 正确副本的到达顺序必须约束提交顺序（公平排序协议的缺口）；(2) 已提交的首个请求必须绑定后续有效性——使冲突的后续请求失效，而非仅记录该请求曾获授权。通过显式的授权见证接口形式化这两个义务，并设计了一个公平的预留/使用（reserve/use）协议来同时满足授权安全性和首到达活性。

Result: 给出了一个公平预留/使用协议，满足授权安全性和首到达活性两个性质。在可信FIFO准入条件下，两个要求会合并，因为准入和执行是原子的；拜占庭SMR将请求提交与使用分离开来。

Conclusion: 在代理授权与隐藏策略状态的拜占庭SMR中，安全实时分配隐藏可消耗资源需要同时满足两条独立要求：公平排序和提交后有效性绑定。通过显式的授权见证接口和公平预留/使用协议，可以同时实现授权安全性与首到达活性。

Abstract: Validated Byzantine SMR assumes that replicas can evaluate the validity of an ordered command. Agent authorization creates a different regime: a command may be valid only relative to a committed policy state that validators cannot reconstruct from the log. A proof that an action was authorized at an old commitment is then only a historical attestation, it does not by itself reserve the hidden resource for later use.
  We isolate two independent requirements for safe live allocation of a hidden consumable resource under a Byzantine leader. First, arrival order at correct replicas must constrain commit order, the gap addressed by fair-ordering protocols. Second, a committed first request must bind later validity: it must make conflicting later requests invalid, not merely record that the first request was once authorized. The second requirement is non-vacuous precisely because the current policy state is hidden and not prefix-recoverable. Using an explicit authorization-witness interface, we characterize the two distinct obligations in this one-shot reservation model and give a fair reserve/use protocol satisfying both authorization safety and first-arrival liveness. Under trusted FIFO admission the two requirements collapse because admission and execution are atomic, Byzantine SMR separates request commitment from use.

</details>


### [83] [Generalizing and accelerating consistency checking for non-transactional distributed storage systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17388)
*Kotikala Raghav,Aman Hassan,Brian Sajeev Kattikat,Patel Jay,RSRS Santhosh,Abhilash Jindal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文泛化了经典的Wing-Gong线性一致性检查算法，使其不仅能检查线性一致性，还能检查其他非事务性一致性保证（如ZooKeeper提供的有序顺序一致性）以及系统特定的、引入额外排序约束的一致性保证。在8个分布式存储系统上的实验表明，该方法易于实现、减少测试误报、帮助调试一致性违规，速度可提升370倍，并能在相同检查时间预算内支持更多并发客户端，共发现6个新的一致性违规bug，其中5个是现有检查器无法发现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性一致性检查器基于经典的Wing-Gong算法，只能检查线性一致性。然而许多分布式存储系统提供的是非事务性的一致性保证（如ZooKeeper的有序顺序一致性）或系统特定的、带有额外排序约束的一致性保证。用线性一致性检查器去检查这些系统会产生误报（false negatives），无法准确测试系统的实际一致性保证。

Method: 将Wing-Gong算法泛化，使其能够处理更广泛的一致性保证模型。通过扩展算法框架，使其支持：(1) 非事务性一致性保证，如有序顺序一致性；(2) 系统特定的一致性保证，即根据系统规范引入额外排序约束的操作历史检查。泛化后的算法能够灵活配置所需的排序约束，从而适配不同系统的一致性语义。

Result: 在8个分布式存储系统上的实验结果显示：该系统特定一致性检查易于实现；减少了测试中的假阴性；有助于调试一致性违规；检查速度可提升高达370倍；在相同的检查时间预算内可扩展到更多并发客户端。此外，报告了6个新的一致性违规bug，其中5个是现有一致性检查器无法发现的。

Conclusion: 泛化Wing-Gong算法是有价值的：它使一致性检查器更加通用和灵活，能够针对不同系统的一致性语义进行定制化检查，显著提升检查效率和能力，并能够发现现有工具无法发现的新的一致性违规bug。

Abstract: Linearizability checkers check if an operation history, observed by concurrent clients, is linearizable. They are used in testing distributed storage systems, and use the classic Wing-Gong (WG) linearizability checking algorithm.
  In this paper, we generalize the WG algorithm to make linearizability checkers more versatile: we can check other non-transactional consistency guarantees, like ordered sequential consistency provided by Zookeeper. Equipped with this generalization, we can also check for system-specific consistency guarantees that introduce additional ordering constraints over operations in a history, as per the system's specification.
  Our experiments with 8 distributed storage systems show that checking for system-specific consistency guarantees is easy to realize, reduces false negatives in testing, helps debug consistency violations, can be up to 370x faster, and can scale to more concurrent clients within the same checking time budget. We report 6 new consistency violation bugs, out of which 5 could not be found with existing consistency checkers.

</details>


### [84] [Optimal Adaptive Multi-Valued Byzantine Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17552)
*Marc Dufay,Anton Paramonov,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Constantinescu等人的拜占庭协议（BA）框架，将原本仅适用于二进制值的结果推广到L-bit值，在同步、部分同步和异步环境下分别提出了更低复杂度（位复杂度和轮复杂度）的拜占庭协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拜占庭协议具有线性延迟和二次消息复杂度，在大规模场景下不实用。Constantinescu等人的工作虽然通过考虑实际拜占庭节点数f来解耦对n和t的依赖，但仅限于二进制值，实际应用中的值通常为多bit，因此需要将其扩展到L-bit值的场景。

Method: 作者利用Constantinescu等人的框架，并结合新颖的技术手段，在最优容错性（同步下t<n/2，否则t<n/3）的前提下，针对不同网络模型分别设计了协议：同步下得到确定性协议（O(n·(L+f·κ))位复杂度，O(f+logn)轮复杂度）；同步和部分同步下得到确定性协议（O(n·κ+t·(L+f·κ))位复杂度，O(f)轮复杂度）；异步下得到期望O(n·κ+t·(L+t·κ))位复杂度和期望O(1)延迟的协议。

Result: 作者在三种网络模型下均获得了将依赖从n和t有效解耦的BA协议：同步环境中最优可实现O(n·(L+f·κ))位复杂度；部分同步和异步环境下复杂度进一步与t·f（或t²）相关而非n·t主导，其中异步协议实现了期望O(1)延迟。

Conclusion: 本文成功将基于实际拜占庭节点数f的复杂度感知BA协议从二进制值扩展到L-bit值，为大规模拜占庭协议的实际应用提供了更高效的解决方案，同时保持了最优容错性。

Abstract: In Byzantine Agreement (BA), $n$ parties, out of which $t$ can be Byzantine, run a distributed protocol to agree on a common valid input. Traditionally, these protocols have a linear latency and quadratic message complexity, making them impractical at a large scale. In their recent work, Constantinescu, Dufay, Paramonov, and Wattenhofer consider the actual number of byzantine parties $f \leq t$ and work toward decoupling the dependency on $n$ and $t$ in the complexity. They obtain a BA protocol with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n + t\cdot f)$ message complexity and $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(f)$ round complexity.
  However, their results are strictly limited to agreement on a binary value. Using the framework given by their work along with novel techniques, we extend these results for BA on an $L$-bit value. With $κ$ being a security parameter, and with optimal resiliency ($t < n/2$ in the synchronous setting or $t < n/3$ otherwise), we obtain:
  - In synchrony, a deterministic protocol with $\mathcal{O}(n\cdot (L + f \cdot κ))$ bit complexity and $\mathcal{O}(f + \log n)$ round complexity.
  - In synchrony and partial synchrony, deterministic protocols with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n \cdot κ+ t\cdot (L + f \cdot κ))$ bit complexity and $\mathcal{O}(f)$ round complexity.
  - In asynchrony, a protocol with $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(n \cdot κ+ t\cdot(L + t \cdot κ))$ expected bit complexity and expected $\mathcal{O}(1)$ latency.

</details>


### [85] [Counting in Population Protocols on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17590)
*Petra Berenbrink,Robert Elsässer,Tom Friedetzky,Thorsten Götte,Lukas Hintze,Dominik Kaaser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究群体协议（population protocol）中节点计数问题。在底层图G上，随机调度器均匀选择边触发状态交互。提出的规模计数协议使用Õ(n)个状态，以高概率在O(B(G)·log²n + L(G)·logn)次交互内稳定，其中B(G)为广播时间，L(G)为负载均衡时间。协议基于两个新子协议：独立随机比特采样（利用调度器确定initiator/responder）和以O(loglogn)加性误差逼近logn（仅用O(poly log n)状态和O(B(G)·log²n)次交互）。精确计数需唯一领导者，其他协议不需要；所有协议无需图G的任何知识。最后给出有/无领导者情形下终止型均匀群体协议计算图规模性质的不可行性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体协议中代理是匿名且相同的，缺乏标识符来打破对称性，这使得确定网络中代理总数成为基础且具挑战性的问题。尽管已有工作研究了完全图上的计数，但针对任意底层图G的计数协议仍缺乏充分的刻画。此外，终止型均匀群体协议的能力边界（尤其在计算图规模性质方面）也未被充分探索，本文旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 本文设计了三个层次的协议：(1) 独立随机比特采样协议，利用随机调度器决定initiator和responder的机制来生成独立随机位；(2) 近似logn协议，仅使用O(poly log n)个状态和O(B(G)·log²n)次交互，达到O(loglogn)加性误差；(3) 精确计数协议，基于前两者并需要唯一领导者，使用Õ(n)个状态。所有协议均不依赖图G的任何知识，并辅以针对终止型均匀群体协议计算图规模性质的不可行性证明。

Result: 精确计数协议以高概率在O(B(G)·log²n + L(G)·logn)次交互内稳定，使用Õ(n)个状态。近似logn协议使用O(poly log n)状态和O(B(G)·log²n)次交互，误差为O(loglogn)。此外，论文证明了在有/无领导者的情况下，终止型均匀群体协议无法计算图规模性质（如节点计数、奇偶性判定），给出了明确的不可行性结果。

Conclusion: 本文为任意底层图上的群体协议提供了首个高效的精确节点计数方案，复杂度以广播时间和负载均衡时间为参数来刻画。同时揭示了终止型均匀群体协议在计算图规模性质上的根本限制，表明在此类模型中精确计数需要非终止或带领导者的协议。这些结果对理解匿名代理分布式计算的能力边界具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: We consider the problem of counting the number of agents in a population protocol where the agents are connected by an underlying graph $G=(V,E)$ with $|V|=n$ nodes. In each step, a random scheduler selects an edge uniformly at random, and the incident nodes make a state transition. As per standard assumptions, agents are identical and anonymous, that is, have no identifiers. To break symmetry, in each interaction one of the agents is declared as the initiator uniformly at random. Our size counting protocol uses $\tilde O(n)$ states and stabilizes in $O( B(G) \cdot \log^2(n) + L(G) \cdot \log(n))$ interactions with high probability, where $B(G)$ is the broadcast time and $L(G)$ is the load balancing time. Our protocol is based on novel protocols for sampling independent random bits (given that the scheduler determines an initiator and responder) and approximating $\log n$ up to an additive error of $O(\log \log n)$ with high probability. The latter uses $O(poly\log(n))$ states and $O(B(G)\cdot\log^2 n)$ interactions. Both results may be of independent interest. The main protocol for exact counting requires the presence of a unique leader, the other two do not. None of the protocols requires any knowledge about the graph $G$. We conclude with impossibility results for terminating uniform population protocols that compute graph-size properties (like counting nodes or determining parity) with and without a leader.

</details>


### [86] [Bounded-State Restoration: Decoupling Local Restore Capacity from External LLM State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17826)
*Zixuan Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出有界状态恢复（BSR）方法，解决层次化KV缓存系统中"保留容量"与"恢复所需本地内存"分离的问题。BSR将完整发现与本地驻留解耦，通过探测完全可复用前缀而不物化整个命中内容，然后通过最多W个块的可复用窗口安装已确认状态，使峰值恢复容量为O(W)而总传输与安装工作为Θ(|S|)。在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上，外部状态从1.956增长至31.277 GiB/rank时，L1 RWS始终为500.75 MiB/rank，实现63.959倍的外部-活跃暂存比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化KV缓存系统可以将长上下文LLM执行状态保留在GPU内存之外的存储层，但保留容量并不能决定使该状态重新可执行所需的本地内存。作者将这一第二资源隔离为"恢复工作集"（Restoration Working Set, RWS）——恢复期间生命周期重叠的峰值本地暂存状态。现有方案（如LMCache全计划路径）需要将完整状态物化到L1才能复用，导致RWS随外部状态规模线性增长。

Method: 提出Bounded-State Restoration（BSR）方法。核心思想是将"完整状态发现"与"本地驻留"分离：BSR首先在不将完整命中物化到L1的前提下探测完全可复用的前缀，然后通过最多W个块的可复用窗口安装已确认状态。在受限制的辅助状态下，峰值恢复容量为O(W)，而总传输与安装工作量仍为Θ(|S|)。由于可复用状态跨异构分配器组和张量并行rank，BSR使用请求级提交规则：部分安装绝不暴露为有效可复用前缀；失败会使声明的前缀失效并回退到较低有效层级或确定性重算。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上（TP=2，跨两个DGX Spark节点），干净的无恢复扫描将外部状态从1.956增长到31.277 GiB/rank，测量得到L1 RWS在W=32时恰好为500.75 MiB/rank，实现了63.959倍的最大状态外部-活跃暂存比。第二次全新的524K token运行重复了最大状态接受结果。评估的层级和rank非对称故障要么暴露完全复用，要么在回退前暴露零外部复用。匹配的SSD优化将512K恢复TTFT从43.1秒降至17.6秒，且不改变RWS。

Conclusion: BSR方法有效解决了层次化KV缓存系统中恢复工作集与外部状态规模解耦的问题，使恢复所需的本地内存保持有界（O(W)），同时不牺牲总传输与安装效率（Θ(|S|)）。实验证明即使外部状态增长超过16倍，L1活跃暂存内存几乎不变，并通过SSD优化在不影响RWS的前提下大幅降低恢复延迟。

Abstract: Hierarchical KV-cache systems can retain long-context LLM execution state beyond GPU memory, but retention capacity does not determine the local memory required to make that state executable again. We isolate this second resource as the restoration working set (RWS): the peak local staging state whose lifetimes overlap during restoration. In the pinned upstream LMCache whole-plan path, measured full-reuse points for 1.956, 7.823, and 15.646 GiB/rank states first succeed at 2, 8, and 16 GiB L1 rungs, with successful L1 peaks of 1.956, 7.824, and 15.648 GiB/rank.
  We introduce Bounded-State Restoration (BSR), which separates complete discovery from local residency. BSR probes the complete reusable prefix without materializing the whole hit in L1, then installs confirmed state through a reusable window of at most $W$ chunks. Under bounded auxiliary state, peak restoration capacity is $O(W)$ while total transfer and installation work remains $Θ(|S|)$. Because reusable state spans heterogeneous allocator groups and tensor-parallel ranks, BSR uses a request-level commit rule: partial installation is never exposed as a valid reusable prefix; failures invalidate the advertised prefix and fall back to a lower valid tier or deterministic recomputation.
  On DeepSeek-V4-Flash with TP=2 across two DGX Spark nodes, a clean no-resume sweep grows external state from 1.956 to 31.277 GiB/rank while measured L1 RWS remains exactly 500.75 MiB/rank at $W=32$, a 63.959x largest-state external-to-live-staging ratio. A second fresh 524K-token run repeats the largest-state acceptance result. Evaluated tier and rank-asymmetric failures expose either complete reuse or zero external reuse before fallback. A matched SSD optimization reduces 512K restore TTFT from 43.1 to 17.6 seconds without changing RWS.

</details>


### [87] [Multivalued Consensus: General Adversaries Require More Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17998)
*Mose Mizrahi,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究n方容错共识中对一般（非门限）对手的下界。基于有限射影几何构造了一族满足Q^d条件的对手结构Z_proj^{n,d}，使得交互一致性需要Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})比特的期望通信，拜占庭协议和广播需要Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})比特。在异步网络中，同样构造使可靠广播和拜占庭协议需要Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})比特，核心集合共识需要Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})比特。作者还设计了非终止的容错可靠广播协议代价为近线性比特，并证明了异步下界的紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究容错共识协议的通信复杂度下界，尤其是针对一般（非门限）对手模型。现有工作主要针对门限对手，而对一般对手结构的通信复杂度下界认识不足。作者希望通过构造特定的对手结构来证明即使在满足Q^d条件（即可容忍的最强对手结构条件）下仍然存在高通信复杂度的协议问题。

Method: 1）利用有限射影几何构造满足Q^d条件的对手结构族Z_proj^{n,d}；2）通过quorum论证（若quorum中非故障方一致输出并终止，则它们之前发送的消息必须足以让外部方也终止）建立异步下界；3）设计非终止的omission-tolerant可靠广播协议（代价(1+1/(δ-1))Ln + O(δn²log(δn))比特）来说明终止性要求是关键；4）给出带终止性的协议使下界达到紧。

Result: 对同步网络：交互一致性需要Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})比特，拜占庭协议和广播需要Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})比特。对异步网络：可靠广播和拜占庭协议需要Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})比特，核心集合共识需要Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})比特。这些下界对send-omission对手成立，即使使用密码学也不能规避。同时，通过非终止协议（近线性通信）和终止协议（O(Ln^{1+1/d}+n²log n)比特）证明异步下界是紧的。

Conclusion: 通过有限射影几何构造的对手结构，建立了同步和异步网络中一般对手模型下共识协议的通信复杂度下界。关键发现是：终止性要求（输出后停止发送消息）是导致高通信代价的本质原因——如果不要求终止，则通信量可以降到近线性。这些结果加深了对容错共识中通信复杂度与对手结构之间关系的理解。

Abstract: We study $n$-party fault-tolerant consensus against general (non-threshold) adversaries. We describe an infinite family $Z_\mathsf{proj}^{n,d}$ of $Q^d$-satisfying $n$-party adversary structures based on finite projective geometry which cause error-free $R$-round protocols for interactive consistency on $L$-bit inputs to require $Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})$ bits of expected communication. Likewise, $Z_\mathsf{proj}^{n,d}$ causes error-free byzantine agreement and broadcast to cost $Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})$ bits. In every case, the lower bound is $Ω(L_{\mathsf{out}} \cdot n^{1+1/d})$ bits, where $L_{\mathsf{out}}$ is the output length.
  The family $Z_\mathsf{proj}^{n,d}$ also causes reliable broadcast and byzantine agreement to cost $Ω(Ln^{1+1/d})$ bits of expected communication in asynchronous networks. Moreover, there exists a related family $Z_\mathsf{2\textsf-proj}^{n,d}$ of $Q^d$-satisfying adversary structures that make core set agreement cost $Ω(Ln^{2+1/d})$ bits. These asynchronous lower bounds hold against send-omission adversaries, even if the protocol uses cryptography. Their basis is that if a quorum of non-faulty parties agree on an output and terminate, then the messages they sent before terminating must suffice for the parties outside the quorum to also terminate with the same output. Surprisingly, if we do not require the parties to terminate (stop sending messages) after they output, then these bounds no longer hold. We show this by designing a non-terminating omission-tolerant reliable broadcast protocol that can for any parameter $δ> 1$ be tuned to cost $(1 + \frac{1}{δ- 1})Ln + O(δn^2\log(δn))$ bits, which is of independent interest. Lastly, we show how to get termination with $O(Ln^{1+1/d} + n^2\log n)$ bits (assuming the $Q^d$ condition), and thus prove our asynchronous lower bounds tight.

</details>


### [88] [Minimizing Commit Rules for DAG-based Atomic Broadcast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18029)
*Petr Kuznetsov,Maxence Perion,Sara Tucci-Piergiovanni*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在无证书的基于轮次的DAG构造上定义了提交规则，并引入子规则关系，据此确定最终同步模型和异步模型下各自的最小提交规则，并提出了基于这些最小提交规则的新协议Minnow（分别实例化为S-Minnow和A-Minnow）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAG-based拜占庭容错原子广播协议的效率取决于其提交规则在DAG上施加的排序条件；条件越少，消息交付延迟越低。然而现有协议并未使用理论上条件最少的安全提交规则，缺乏对最小提交规则的系统性刻画和证明。

Method: 在无证书的轮次制DAG构造上定义了提交规则以及子规则关系（若cr1提交的顶点集合包含cr2提交的所有顶点，则cr1是cr2的子规则）。利用该偏序关系，分别在最终同步模型和异步模型中推导出需对DAG施加最少条件即可安全提交任意顶点集合的最小提交规则，并基于此设计Minnow协议。

Result: 在最终同步模型和异步模型中分别确定了最小提交规则，证明现有DAG-based原子广播协议均未使用这些最小规则，并提出了可在这两种模型下实例化的新协议Minnow（S-Minnow与A-Minnow）。

Conclusion: 通过子规则关系系统化地比较DAG提交规则，可以找到对应模型下的最小（最优）提交规则，从而在保证安全性的前提下最大化协议效率；Minnow验证了这些最小规则在实际协议中的可行性。

Abstract: A popular class of Byzantine fault-tolerant atomic broadcast protocols rely on directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) that represent causal relations between broadcast messages. Each process applies a protocol-specific \emph{commit rule} on its local DAG to determine which vertices can be delivered in a total order. Intuitively, commit rules that impose fewer conditions on the DAG to order its vertices imply more efficient protocols, as the broadcast messages are delivered with lower latency.
  In this paper, we define commit rules on an uncertified round-based DAG construction, and introduce a sub-rule relation between them: a commit rule $cr_1$ is a sub-rule of a commit rule $cr_2$ if, for any DAG, the set of vertices committed by $cr_1$ contains all vertices committed by $cr_2$. We use the sub-rule relation to determine a minimal commit rule in an eventually synchronous model and a minimal commit rule in an asynchronous model, namely, two commit rules that require the least conditions on the DAG to safely commit any set of vertices in their respective models. To the best of our knowledge, no existing DAG-based atomic broadcast protocol relies on such minimal commit rules: we introduce \emph{Minnow}, a new protocol for DAG-based atomic broadcast, which can be instantiated in both eventually synchronous (S-Minnow) and asynchronous networks (A-Minnow).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GxP-Agent，一种将监管流程顺序编码为有向无环图（DAG）的多智能体系统，用于临床试编程中的CDISC标准数据分析数据集生成。在CDISC-Bench基准测试中，GxP-Agent配合Claude Sonnet 4.6实现了100%的结构匹配（49个变量、254条记录），远超最佳基线（59.2%），且DAG拓扑使弱模型GPT-4.1也能达到59.2%的正确率，而单智能体架构下为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试编程（将研究方案转化为符合CDISC标准的分析就绪数据集）是监管提交的瓶颈环节。现有的基于LLM的代码生成在此任务上灾难性失败——在11次单次尝试中，5个前沿模型无一能生成有效的受试者级分析数据集。这揭示了仅依赖LLM推理能力不足以处理需要严格监管流程顺序的领域任务。

Method: GxP-Agent采用多智能体架构，将监管流程顺序编码为有向无环图（DAG），把整体数据集生成任务分解为15个领域特定节点。每个节点由携带pharmaverse技能上下文的worker agent执行，并配备验证门控和条件重试机制。该系统基于FDA试点提交的CDISCPilot01数据构建了新的执行型基准CDISC-Bench。

Result: 在CDISC-Bench基准上，GxP-Agent配合Claude Sonnet 4.6在三次独立运行中实现100%结构匹配（49/49个变量，254条正确记录），而最佳检索增强基线为59.2%，所有单智能体和扁平多智能体方案为0%。弱模型GPT-4.1在DAG架构下达到59.2%的结构匹配（其他架构下为0%）。该方法还泛化到ADAE（不良事件，9节点分支DAG，55个变量，1,191条记录），首次尝试即实现100%结构匹配。

Conclusion: 将领域过程知识编码为图拓扑结构——而非单纯依赖LLM推理——是实现可靠、符合GxP合规的临床试编程的关键推动因素。DAG架构不仅提升了最强模型的表现，还使较弱模型在原本完全失败的任务上获得了有意义的能力，表明过程知识的结构化编码比模型原始能力更为重要。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [90] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Aegis，一个运行时治理系统，将AI智能体的模型输出视为"操作提案"，在工具执行前通过可信决策层进行调解。在评估的沙箱语料库中，Aegis实现了零违规工具应用和零风险副作用完成，但作者明确声明结果仅支持较窄的系统级声明，不证明通用自主智能体安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统请求工具操作（如修改文件、发送消息、启动作业或改变工作流状态），将安全问题的范式从有害文本生成转变为有害操作副作用。提示级别的治理只能塑造模型行为，无法创建执行边界，因此需要一种新的运行时治理机制来建立可信的执行控制层。

Method: Aegis采用"模型提议，可信运行时决策"的架构：模型输出被视为动作提案，通过可信决策层调解后再执行工具。具体机制包括：1）根据活跃策略状态评估提案；2）服务端解析溯源（provenance）；3）不确定性下默认失败关闭（fail closed）；4）对选定案例路由至Senate式结算路径——一种基于法定人数（quorum）的非单边授权机制。通过重复沙箱语料库进行评估，覆盖五个运行族、42个任务、三种条件和每族十次重复。

Result: 在6,300行数据中，仅提示策略调节产生了79条风险比较器路径泄漏；在2,100行Aegis治理数据中，零次治理模拟工具应用和零次治理风险副作用完成。所有1,832条Aegis尝试的治理行保留了可信的Aegis解析溯源，所有1,019条Senate结算行都有法定人数和最终签名计数证据。

Conclusion: 结果表明运行时操作边界治理在评估的沙箱语料库中有效防止了观察到的风险提案转化为治理副作用。但作者明确限定：这些结果不支持通用自主智能体安全的结论，仅支持在该评估语料库中运行时操作边界治理的最小系统级声明。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [91] [The Price of Thinking: Reasoning Effort as a Model-Specific API Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16956)
*Yeabin Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过注册的配对对比实验，比较了显式高推理努力合同与省略努力条款合同在Sonnet 5上的表现差异（30道AIME 2026题目，每题5次调用）。结果显示显式高努力合同每次调用平均成本高出\$0.01031，但未检测到显著的准确性差异（+0.0133，置信区间包含0且上限为+0.0467）。每正确回答成本在高努力合同下为\$0.08665，低于省略合同下的\$0.07662。研究还通过模型元数据和原始响应探针记录了模型特定（包括供应商内部）的省略语义，所有声称均受限于所研究的模型、任务和采集日期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API买家购买的是带日期的合同而非单纯的模型名称，合同包含推理努力条款等多项内容。推理努力条款（尤其显式高努力与省略之间的差异）对成本和准确性的实际影响尚不清楚，需要严格的对照实证研究来回答该问题。

Method: 采用注册的配对对比设计：对Sonnet 5分别使用显式高推理努力合同与省略努力条款合同，在30道AIME 2026题目上各进行5次调用。每次付费尝试分配一个冻结的终态类别，推理阶段对题目进行重采样但保留重复调用。所有请求注册表、解析器、终态分类体系、统计计划和分析管线均在查看结果前冻结。

Result: 显式高努力合同每次调用平均成本比省略合同高出\$0.01031（95%CI: [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]）；准确性对比为+0.0133（95%CI: [-0.0267, +0.0467]），未检测到显著差异，但区间允许高达4.67个百分点的增益无法被排除。每正确回答成本：高努力合约\$0.08665 vs 省略合约\$0.07662。模型元数据揭示了模型特定的省略语义。

Conclusion: 显式指定高推理努力会稳定增加API成本，但本设计未能检测到相应的准确性提升；省略推理努力条款的语义因模型而异（甚至在同一供应商内），购买者需了解具体合同的省略语义。所有结论均限定于所研究的模型、任务和采集日期，不可过度泛化。

Abstract: API buyers purchase a dated contract, not a model name alone: the contract includes the requested and served model, reasoning-effort term or its omission, output rail, service product, prompt, and price schedule. We study the reasoning-effort term through a registered paired contrast of Sonnet 5 with explicit high effort against the same model with effort omitted, using 30 AIME 2026 items and five calls per item. Every paid attempt was assigned one frozen terminal category, and inference resampled items while retaining their repeated calls. Mean delivered cost was \$0.01031 per call higher under the explicit-high contract than under the omitted contract [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]. The corresponding accuracy contrast was +0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]; we did not detect an accuracy difference, and the interval permits a gain of up to 4.67 percentage points that this design cannot rule out. Cost per correct answer was \$0.08665 under the high-effort contract and \$0.07662 under the omitted contract, as registered point estimates. A dated contract census, Models-API metadata, and preregistered raw-response probes further documented model-specific omission semantics, including within a provider; claims remained at documentation grade when raw structure was indeterminate. The request registry, parser, terminal taxonomy, statistical plan, and analysis pipeline were frozen before outcomes were examined; the resulting claims are bounded to the model, task, and collection date studied.

</details>


### [92] [FedPref: Federated Preference Learning for Structured Radiology Report Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16971)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedPref是一种联邦偏好学习框架，利用冻结的公开语言模型生成候选JSON提取，再由本地标注排序，各机构仅共享紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器模型更新。在六家模拟医院的不均衡数据场景下，FedPref相比各站点独立训练将客户端平均F1提升2.49点，最差站点F1提升9.10点，且无需共享报告或标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告以自由文本描述发现和位置，但下游检索与分析需要固定模式的关系提取。不同机构间的标注数据分布不均：小型医院本地证据少，而跨机构数据池化可能不可行（数据隐私或合规限制）。因此需要一个无需共享原始数据即可协作学习关系提取的方法。

Method: FedPref采用三阶段流程：(1) 冻结的公开语言模型作为异构教师池，针对每份报告提出多种替代JSON提取候选；(2) 本地机构标注对候选进行排名（偏好数据）；(3) 各站点协同训练紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器，仅共享模型更新而非原始报告或标注。异构教师池提供跨模型对比，以缓解单模型重复采样导致的坍缩问题。

Result: 在六个模拟医院（数据量及疾病流行率不均衡）的开发集上，FedPref相比各站点隔离训练，客户端平均F1提升2.49点（最差站点提升9.10点），数据量最小的站点获益最大。中间训练（在池化的偏好对并集上）的客户端平均F1较FedPref高2.66点。在锁定的400份报告人工验证金标准测试集上，FedPref取得68.68 F1，池化训练为71.67，保持了相同的排序，确认了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: FedPref证明了在数据不均衡且无法池化的多机构场景下，通过仅共享模型更新和偏好排序即可实现有效的协作式关系提取。该方法使数据最少的机构获益最大，同时避免了共享报告或标注的隐私问题，为医疗影像报告的联邦学习提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings and locations in free text, but downstream search and analysis require these relations in a fixed schema. Learning this extraction requires labels that are unevenly distributed across institutions: smaller hospitals have less local evidence, and pooling data may be infeasible. We introduce FedPref: frozen public language models propose alternative JSON extractions, local annotations rank them, and sites collaboratively train compact Qwen3-8B adapters while sharing only model updates. A heterogeneous teacher pool provides cross-model contrast when repeated single-model samples collapse. On development data from six simulated hospitals with unequal data volume and disease prevalence, FedPref improves client-mean F1 by 2.49 points and worst-site F1 by 9.10 points compared with training each site in isolation, with the largest gains at the sites holding the least data. Central training on the pooled preference-pair union is 2.66 points higher on client-mean F1. On a locked, 400-report manually validated gold test set, FedPref reaches 68.68 F1 and pooled training 71.67, preserving that same ordering. FedPref thus lets institutions with unequal, unpooled data benefit from collaboration without ever sharing reports or annotations.

</details>


### [93] [The Problem Is the Problem: Towards Scalable Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16977)
*Zeyu Zheng,Shengtong Zhang,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的人机协作数学发现范式FAR（Find, Attempt, Recommend），将人类输入从预先选定的单一问题转变为研究方向，通过文献检索、候选猜想筛选、推理和自动分诊等多级级联流程，自动化问题搜索并聚焦人类评审注意力。在组合学试点中，从5,245篇论文中恢复6,453个候选猜想，经过多轮过滤后选出77项供作者团队评审，发现了多个重要数学结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI辅助数学发现流程中，人类精力主要集中在前端（选题）和后端（审阅），这两个阶段正成为研究级数学的瓶颈。前沿模型推理和专家评审都是稀缺资源，如何合理分配这些资源是提升AI辅助数学发现效率的核心问题。

Method: 提出了FAR（Find, Attempt, Recommend）级联流水线，受搜索和推荐系统启发。系统从广泛文献语料库中搜索研究方向内的候选问题，依次经历问题过滤阶段（筛选形式良好且仍然开放的猜想）、推理与自动分诊阶段（识别潜在解决），最终将已通过多级过滤的产物聚焦到人类评审。人类输入由单一预先选择的问题变为研究方向。

Result: 在组合学试点中，流水线从5,245篇论文中恢复6,453个候选猜想或开放问题，过滤至4,717个形式良好且仍开放的问题，进一步通过推理和自动分诊阶段选出598个潜在解决方案，最终77项进入作者团队评审。识别出多个有趣发现，包括Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts、Erdős--Straus、Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova和Lund--Saraf--Wolf等猜想和问题的相关结果。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了这种新的人机协作模式对数学发现的有效性，即通过自动化文献搜索和级联过滤来定向分配稀缺的推理与评审资源，能够显著提升AI辅助数学研究的效率。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of contributing to mathematical research. In research practice, frontier-model reasoning is a limited resource, and expert mathematical review is even more sharply constrained. Allocating these scarce resources well is therefore central to making AI-assisted mathematical discovery efficient. In most current AI-for-math workflows, human effort is concentrated at the beginning and end, in selecting suitable research problems and later reviewing the resulting artifacts. These two stages are becoming bottlenecks for research-level mathematics. We address them by proposing a new human-AI discovery paradigm. The human input is no longer a single problem selected in advance, but a research direction in which the experts have interest and expertise. The system then searches a broad literature corpus for candidate problems in that direction. Inspired by search and recommender systems, we build Find, Attempt, and Recommend (FAR), a literature-to-review cascade that automates the search for suitable problems and focuses human attention on artifacts that have passed several stages of filtering. In a combinatorics pilot, the pipeline starts from 5,245 combinatorics papers, recovers 6,453 candidate conjectures or open problems, and filters them to 4,717 apparently well-posed and still-open conjectures. Subsequent reasoning and automated triage stages surface 598 potential resolutions and select 77 items for author-team review. Among them, we identify many interesting discoveries, including results on conjectures and questions of Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts, Erdős--Straus, Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova, and Lund--Saraf--Wolf. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of this new mode of human-AI collaboration for mathematical discovery.

</details>


### [94] [SkillEffect: Checked Lowering for Memory-Bounded Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17007)
*Yinuo Wang,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillEffect是一个"受检降级"（checked-lowering）运行时，用于执行Agent技能中的工具调用，确保在执行授权前由独立检查器验证每个提议的低级实现（lowering）的内存约束。通过插件化的关系合约（源识别器、输入事实提取器、有界IR构造器、竞技场边界函数、后置条件），它在六种算子家族和六种插件中显著降低峰值内存并提高在固定容量上限下的完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能可以指定工具使用的过程和资源义务，语言模型将其实例化为具体程序。然而，当模型将这些指导转换为现有工具接口的代码时，即使语义上正确的程序也可能加载整个输入，超出单次工具调用的可用内存。这导致Agent在资源受限环境下执行工具调用时存在内存溢出风险。

Method: SkillEffect采用受检降级架构：在执行授权之前，独立检查器从提交的程序和不可变输入重建每个提议的低级实现。每个关系插件提供源识别器、输入事实提取器、有界IR构造器、竞技场边界函数和后置条件。共享运行时提供检查选择、有界VM执行、原子容量租赁和分阶段发布。通用性是架构性的而非自动化的——每个受支持的计算需要审计过的关系插件，而调度、资源控制、执行和发布机制跨插件共享。

Result: 在六种算子家族中，有界访问显著降低了峰值内存，并提高了在外部固定容量限制下的完成率。六个插件在五种执行模式（从流式归约到有界堆Top-k）中实例化了同一合约。XLSX入职研究和Top-k扩展表明，新关系和新保留状态模式可复用同一信任边界，检查器接受所有评估的合法配置并拒绝所有对抗性提案。

Conclusion: 一种受检降级架构能够在Agent工具调度时强制实施异构的注册内存关系，为语言模型实例化的工具程序提供可审计的内存安全保证，同时保持架构的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Agent Skills can specify procedural and resource obligations for tool use, and language models instantiate them as concrete programs. However, when models turn this guidance into code for existing tool interfaces, even a semantically correct program may load an entire input and exceed the memory available to one tool call. We present SkillEffect, a checked-lowering runtime for computations with a recoverable source relation, an audited bounded implementation, and a registered output postcondition. Before granting execution authority, an independent checker rebuilds each proposed lowering from the submitted program and immutable input. Every relation plugin supplies a source recognizer, input-fact extractor, bounded-IR constructor, arena-bound function, and postcondition; one common runtime provides checked selection, bounded-VM execution, atomic capacity leasing, and staged publication. Generality in SkillEffect is architectural rather than automatic: each supported computation requires an audited relation plugin, while the dispatch, resource-control, execution, and publication mechanisms are shared across plugins. Across six operator families, bounded access substantially reduces peak memory and improves completion under externally fixed caps. Six plugins instantiate the same contract across five execution patterns, from streaming reduction to bounded-heap Top-k. The XLSX onboarding study and Top-k extension show that a new relation and a new retained-state pattern reuse the same trust boundary, while the checker accepts all evaluated legal configurations and rejects all adversarial proposals. Together, these results show that one checked-lowering architecture can enforce heterogeneous registered memory relations at Agent tool dispatch.

</details>


### [95] [Memory Is Communication: The Frontier Between Remembering and Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17053)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究有限资源（记忆与消息速率）约束下，有界智能体如何分配信息预算以在自身历史与同伴信息之间做出最优权衡，提出了"记忆—信号前沿"（remembering-signaling frontier）概念，并通过参照游戏实验提出假设：当历史信息能带来更大的任务损失降低时，智能体所需的同伴通信更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的决策者（无论是生物还是AI系统）都面临记忆容量和通信带宽的双重限制。理解在这两种资源约束下如何最优分配信息预算，对于设计高效协作系统、理解自然智能体的通信行为具有重要意义。然而现有研究缺乏一个统一的框架来描述记忆与通信之间的权衡关系。

Method: 本文提出一个理论框架：在给定任务和决策规则下，记忆与消息速率对达到性能阈值的组合构成一个可行区域（achievable region），其高效边界被定义为"记忆—信号前沿"。在此基础上，通过初步的参照游戏（referential games）实验来验证假设——比较目标重复条件下与隐藏周期性规则条件下消息长度的差异，并计划通过改变记忆和消息速率的实验来估计该前沿。

Result: 初步参照游戏实验表明：目标重复出现时成功的消息更短，而由隐藏循环规则带来的可预测性并未使消息变短。这一结果为"历史带来的损失降低越大则所需的同伴通信越少"的假设提供了初步支持证据。

Conclusion: 论文提出了记忆与通信之间的系统性权衡框架（记忆—信号前沿），给出了初步实验证据，并指出未来可通过系统变化记忆和消息速率的实验来更精确地估计该前沿并验证跨协作任务的预测。

Abstract: A bounded agent may obtain information for a decision from its own past, from peers, or from both sources. Retaining task-relevant history can reduce later communication, while a peer message can supply what memory lacks. Under limits on both resources, how should an agent allocate its information budget? Given a fixed task and decision rule, the memory and message rate pairs attaining a performance threshold form an achievable region under specified rules for using history and peer observations. We call its efficient boundary the remembering--signaling frontier. Across conditions where history permits the same maximum reduction in task loss, we hypothesize that a bounded agent will need less peer communication when it obtains a larger loss reduction from history. In preliminary referential games, target repetition coincided with shorter successful messages, while predictability from a hidden cyclic rule did not shorten them. Experiments varying memory and message rates can estimate the frontier and test this prediction across cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [96] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiSCO是一种零样本、严格黑盒的文本到图像生成模型安全防御方法，通过在提示词层面进行基于对比评分的分布引导后缀扩展，在不访问模型内部的情况下降低NSFW内容生成成功率，分别将未防御和已防御模型在I2P基准上的ASR降低37.7%和25.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型存在生成NSFW内容的安全隐患，尤其是面对红队对抗攻击时问题更为严重。现有防御方法大多依赖白盒假设（如文本编码器优化、权重编辑或推理时干预），无法扩展到专有模型。黑盒替代方案基于LLM提示重写，但在作者识别的"良性对抗"问题（语言上安全但仍触发有害生成的提示）上失效。

Method: DiSCO提出一种完全在提示词层面运行的零样本、严格黑盒防御模块，通过束搜索（beam search）进行分布引导的后缀扩展，利用目标模型自身生成的安全与不安全图像池进行对比评分优化，并迭代自适应反馈直到产生安全内容。

Result: 在I2P基准上，DiSCO使未防御模型的有害生成成功率（ASR）降低37.7%，使已防御模型的ASR降低25.13%，同时保持了语义保真度并改善了图像一致性。作为黑盒、架构无关的模块，DiSCO可直接应用于任何文本到图像系统。

Conclusion: DiSCO有效解决了现有防御方法在白盒依赖和"良性对抗"问题上的局限，提供了一种即插即用、无需模型重训练或微调的黑盒安全防御方案，可广泛适用于各类专有文本到图像生成系统。

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{benign adversarial} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>


### [97] [KernelArc: A Multi-Agent Framework for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17071)
*Joyjit Kundu,Ben Stoffelen,Kaili Wang,Peter Vrancx,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KernelArc，一个面向异构工作负载的多智能体GPU内核优化框架。策略特化的智能体并行运行，通过仅结论共享内存、确定性基准守卫和只读跨智能体状态（含高原触发草稿机制）进行协调。在NVIDIA H100和B200上使用SOL-ExecBench基准评估，生成的实现覆盖自定义BF16 GEMM、cuBLASLt Expert配置表、融合MoE反向、形状门控解码器融合、原生NVFP4 GQA和分页prefill注意力等。在2026年7月30日的SOL-ExecBench公开排行榜快照中，提交在代表性L1、L2、Quantization和FlashInfer任务上排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化是异构工作负载下极具挑战性的问题，单个优化方法难以覆盖多样性任务；共享多智能体搜索有望在固定候选预算内拓宽探索空间、达到更强的优化结果。

Method: KernelArc采用多智能体框架：策略特化的智能体并行运行，通过仅结论共享内存协调，配备确定性基准守卫防止作弊，以及只读跨智能体状态配合高原触发的草稿机制来促进信息共享与探索。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100和B200上、基于SOL-ExecBench代表性工作负载的评估中，生成的实现覆盖了从自定义BF16 GEMM、静态cuBLASLt Expert配置表、融合MoE反向到原生NVFP4分组查询注意力和分页prefill注意力等广泛领域。在2026年7月30日的公开排行榜快照中，这些提交在L1、L2、Quantization和FlashInfer代表性任务上均排名第一。

Conclusion: 实验轨迹支持论文核心论点：共享多智能体搜索能在固定候选预算下拓宽探索范围并达到更强的基线解，而各协调特征（仅结论共享、确定性基准守卫、只读状态与高原触发草稿）的价值取决于具体的内核类型和优化阶段，并非所有特征在所有场景下均有益。

Abstract: We present KernelArc, a multi-agent framework for autonomous GPU kernel optimization across heterogeneous workloads. Strategy-specialized agents run in parallel and coordinate through conclusions-only shared memory, a deterministic benchmark guard, and read-only cross-agent state with plateau-triggered drafting. We evaluate \kernelarc{} on NVIDIA H100 and B200 GPUs using category-representative SOL-ExecBench workloads. The resulting implementations span custom BF16 GEMM, static cuBLASLt Expert-API configuration tables, fused mixture-of-experts backward, shape-gated decoder-layer fusion, native NVFP4 grouped-query attention, and paged prefill attention. At the public SOL-ExecBench leaderboard snapshot recorded on July~30, 2026, these submissions ranked first on representative L1, L2, Quantization, and FlashInfer tasks. The trajectories support the paper's central motivation: shared multi-agent search can broaden exploration and reach stronger incumbents within a fixed candidate budget, while the value of individual coordination features depends on the kernel and optimization stage.

</details>


### [98] [A decodability criterion predicts when hidden-state selection beats majority voting in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17124)
*Zhixiang wang,Ziliang Hong,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE（Correctness-Axis SElection），一种基于模型隐藏状态训练的线性门控选择器，用于动态选择LLM采样答案中最优候选。核心贡献是“decodability（可解码性）”指标，它能无泄漏地预测隐藏状态选择是否优于多数投票，在未见数据上相关系数r=0.75，并在中等难度和高难度问题上分别提升最多19和16.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票在困难问题上不可靠，因为采样答案共享相关错误导致错误答案胜出；而基于隐藏状态读取正确性信号进行候选选择虽然前景较好，但其准确率因模型和任务而异，且缺乏衡量何时可信任该方法的指标。

Method: 提出CASE组合器：在答案token的隐藏状态上训练线性门控，为每个候选打分并选择最高分候选。同时提出decodability指标，一种无泄漏的度量，评估门控将正确候选排在错误候选之上的能力；采用问题分组（question-grouped）评估以避免问题身份（question-identity）泄漏。

Result: 在保留数据上，decodability对选择优于投票的准确率增益预测的Pearson相关系数r=0.75，决策阈值接近AUC=0.60。在通用和医学LLM上，CASE在中等难度问题上最多提升19个百分点，在困难问题上提升16.8个百分点。decodability取决于模型需回忆的对齐知识而非模型规模，且其预测可在未见科学领域内迁移（误差在3.8个点以内）。

Conclusion: decodability为给定模型和任务提供了可在事前测量的实用准则，用于在“学习式选择”与“多数投票”之间做出决策。

Abstract: Combining the answers a large language model (LLM) samples for a question into one decision is a test-time information fusion problem, usually solved by majority voting. Voting is unreliable on difficult questions, where the sampled answers share correlated errors, so the wrong answer can win and drawing more samples makes the decision worse. Selecting a candidate by reading a correctness signal from the model's hidden states is a promising alternative, but its accuracy varies across models and tasks, and no measure indicates when it can be trusted. In this paper, we propose CASE (Correctness-Axis SElection), a dynamic selection combiner that trains a linear gate on the answer-token hidden state and selects the highest-scoring candidate. Its main contribution is decodability, a leakage-free measure of how well the gate ranks a question's correct candidates above its incorrect ones, which predicts whether hidden-state selection will outperform voting. A conventional probe appears accurate only because of question-identity leakage, which vanishes under question-grouped evaluation. On held-out data, decodability predicts the accuracy gain of selection over voting with a Pearson correlation r=0.75 and a decision threshold near AUC=0.60. Across general and medical LLMs, CASE improves over voting by up to 19 points on medium-difficulty questions and 16.8 points on hard questions. Decodability depends on the aligned knowledge a model must recall, not on its scale, and its prediction transfers to an unseen scientific domain within 3.8 points. It thus provides a practical criterion, measurable in advance for a given model and task, for choosing between learned selection and majority voting.

</details>


### [99] [Toward Personal Intelligence Through Cooperative Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17128)
*Yashar Talebirad,Osman Jime,Ali Parsaee,Eden Redman,Yongbin Kim,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"合作式观察"（cooperative observation）框架，认为个人AI系统通过部分建模用户目标并在用户评价、同意和控制的反馈循环中优化其观察渠道，从而实现更有用的个性化智能。基于Organizm原型六个月的初步单主体报告，文章概述了衡量观察质量如何影响个人AI的评估方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI系统需要有用户目标、约束和持续承诺的模型才能代表用户规划和行动，而模型质量受系统能观察到的信息上限约束。但更广泛的观察并不能自动改善协助，因为有界系统必须为当前任务选择和压缩信息。作者希望揭示这一观察瓶颈的合作结构，从而为个人智能提供理论框架。

Method: 作者提出合作式观察框架，描述系统构建用户生活部分模型、用户评估其行动、用户同意与控制塑造未来观察通道之间的反馈循环。采用Organizm原型进行为期六个月的初步单主体研究，并据此规划评估方法以测量观察质量对个人AI性能的影响。

Result: 论文报告了Organizm原型的初步单主体使用情况（六个月的纵向观察），并概述了未来评估方向，旨在量化观察质量（如观察广度、压缩选择性）如何影响个人AI的协助效果和用户信任。

Conclusion: 有用的、可检视的行为能给用户理由维持或扩展观察通道，而失败则可能导致用户纠正、收窄、撤销或放弃该通道。合作式观察作为个人智能的框架，强调了有用性、信任与未来访问之间的动态循环关系。

Abstract: A personal AI system needs a model of the user's goals, constraints, and ongoing commitments to plan and act on their behalf, and the quality of that model is bounded by what the system can observe. Broader observation does not by itself improve assistance because a bounded system must select and compress information for the task at hand. We argue that this observation bottleneck has a cooperative structure: the system builds a partial model of the user's changing life, the user evaluates its actions, and the user's consent and control shape what it can observe next. Useful and inspectable behavior can give users a reason to maintain or expand the observation channel, while failures can lead them to correct, narrow, revoke, or abandon it. We use the term cooperative observation for this feedback loop among usefulness, trust, and future access, and propose it as a framework for personal intelligence. We report a preliminary single-subject account from Organizm, a prototype used over six months, and outline evaluation directions for measuring how observation quality shapes personal AI.

</details>


### [100] [KnowSim: Evaluating Information Calibration in LLM Assistants with User Simulators that Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17150)
*Yoonjoo Lee,Hyoungwook Jin,Tae Soo Kim,Shaoyang Zhang,Philippe Laban,Q. Vera Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KNOWSIM评估框架，通过显式建模用户知识状态的用户模拟器来衡量LLM的信息校准能力。该模拟器将知识表示为带有前置关系的信息单元图，并基于学习理论更新知识状态，计算知识增益、交付校准和认知过载三个指标。在705个跨领域人机会话中验证，排名与人类判断显著一致（73-74%符号一致性），优于三个基线模拟器。对9个LLM的评估揭示最佳模型随用户知识水平而变化，呈现出标准评估无法发现的aptitude-treatment交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在知识密集型任务中需要根据用户不断变化的理解和认知能力来校准信息，但现有的用户模拟器没有显式建模用户知识，无法产生跨知识水平的真实交互，也无法反映知识演化过程中的交互动态。

Method: KNOWSIM框架构建了一个维护显式知识状态的用户模拟器：将知识表示为带有前置关系的信息单元图，在基于学习理论设定的更新规则下随交互演化。从知识状态轨迹中计算三个指标：知识增益（Knowledge Gain）、交付校准（Delivery Calibration）、认知过载（Cognitive Overload）。

Result: 在705个人机会话（覆盖两个领域，按知识水平分层）上进行验证，KNOWSIM的排名与人类判断显著对齐（73-74%符号一致性），优于三个基线模拟器。将其应用于9个LLM后，发现最佳模型随用户知识水平而变，揭示了标准评估无法捕捉的aptitude-treatment交互效应。

Conclusion: 显式建模用户知识状态的模拟器能更真实地反映信息校准过程，且基于知识状态轨迹的指标比传统基线模拟器更符合人类判断。标准评估掩盖了不同用户知识水平下LLM表现的差异，KNOWSIM揭示的aptitude-treatment交互对模型选择和评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: To effectively collaborate with users on knowledge-intensive tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) must perform information calibration: matching content to a user's evolving understanding and cognitive capacity. Yet user simulators used to evaluate and train LLMs do not explicitly model user knowledge so they neither produce realistic interactions across knowledge levels nor reflect how interactions unfold as that knowledge evolves. To close this gap, we introduce KNOWSIM, an evaluation framework built around a user simulator that maintains explicit knowledge states, represented as a graph of Information Units with prerequisite relationships, that evolve under update rules grounded in learning theory. KNOWSIM computes three metrics (Knowledge Gain, Delivery Calibration, Cognitive Overload) directly from the knowledge state trajectory, reflecting key mechanistic aspects of information calibration. We validate KNOWSIM against 705 human-AI sessions across two domains, stratified by knowledge level: its rankings align significantly with human judgments (73-74% sign agreement), outperforming three baseline simulators. Applied to 9 LLMs, KNOWSIM reveals that the best model shifts by user knowledge level, revealing aptitude-treatment interactions invisible to standard evaluation.

</details>


### [101] [Synthesizing Feature Extractors: An Agentic Approach for Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17170)
*Hai Xia,Carlos Ansótegui,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型（LLM）在"检查-修复-验证"智能体循环中自动合成可执行Python脚本的方法，用于为约束满足问题（CSP）生成可解释的、针对特定问题的特征提取器。该方法从MiniZinc模型和实例出发，生成代码构建类型化图表示并计算结构属性（图密度、变量聚类、约束紧密度等）。在三个组合问题上（车辆路径、汽车排序、固定长度纠错码）评估，合成特征提取器在算法选择上持续优于专家手工设计的mzn2feat特征（FLECC上测试集准确率最高提升8.3个百分点）和基于transformer的trans2feat变体，同时保持可检查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束满足问题的算法选择依赖于提取能刻画问题结构的特征，但手工设计特征提取器需要深厚的领域专业知识，且当新问题类型出现时会迅速成为瓶颈。现有方法（如mzn2feat和trans2feat）要么依赖专家手工构建，要么使用不可解释的transformer模型，缺乏自动化且可解释的特征提取手段。

Method: 该方法利用LLM作为智能体，运行"检查-修复-验证"循环来合成特征提取器。给定高层MiniZinc模型和实例，LLM生成Python代码，代码构建类型化图表示并计算结构属性（如图密度、变量聚类和约束紧密度）。生成的提取器作为算法选择器的输入特征，与包含五种最先进求解器的求解器组合搭配使用。

Result: 在三个组合问题（车辆路由、汽车排序、固定长度纠错码）上的实验表明，合成的特征提取器在算法选择上始终优于专家手工构建的mzn2feat特征（在FLECC上测试集准确率最高提升8.3个百分点），也优于最好的基于transformer的trans2feat变体。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动化特征提取方法能够在无人工干预下生成高质量、可解释的CSP特征提取器，既提升了算法选择性能，又保持了特征的可检查性，为解决新问题类别的特征工程设计瓶颈提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Algorithm selection for constraint satisfaction problems requires extracting features that capture problem structure. Manually designing feature extractors demands deep domain expertise and quickly becomes a bottleneck when new problem classes appear. We present an automated approach that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) in an agentic check--fix--verify loop to synthesize executable Python scripts that act as interpretable, problem-specific feature extractors. Given a high-level MiniZinc model and an instance, the LLM agent generates code that constructs a typed graph representation and computes structural properties such as graph density, variable clustering, and constraint tightness. We evaluate our approach on three combinatorial problems (vehicle routing, car sequencing, fixed-length error-correcting codes) with a portfolio of five state-of-the-art solvers. The synthesized extractors yield algorithm selectors that consistently outperform both expert-curated mzn2feat features (up to $8.3$ percentage points (pp) test-set accuracy on FLECC) and the best transformer-based trans2feat variants. In the meanwhile, the synthesized feature extractors remain inspectable.

</details>


### [102] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Automated Safety Benchmarks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17183)
*Nyamtulla Shaik,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对26个开源小语言模型（SLM）在5个主流安全基准套件上的评估发现，现有面向大语言模型设计的安全基准难以可靠地迁移到SLM上，模糊判断（ambiguous judgments）占主导且与提示复杂度和模型架构相关，表明LLM-centric安全基准不能作为SLM安全评估的独立证据；此外，模糊性普遍存在导致基于聚合均值的排行榜在数学上不稳定，模型排名会因模糊性处理方式不同而发生显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM在资源受限和隐私敏感场景中部署越来越广泛，其安全与偏见失效可能带来安全和社会的风险。然而现有的AI安全/合规基准是为大语言模型设计的，可能无法可靠地迁移到SLM上，因此需要系统性评估这些基准对SLM评估的有效性和可靠性。

Method: 作者对5个广泛使用的基准套件在26个开源SLM上进行了大规模评估，采用统一的评判标准（harmful=0，safe=1，ambiguous/irrelevant=0.5）。同时分析了模糊率与提示复杂度、模型架构、词汇密度、输出困惑度、输出长度、词汇复杂度、自一致性、回复-提示相似度等因素的相关性，并在不同模糊性处理策略下检验了排行榜排名的稳健性。

Result: 发现模糊判断在各基准中占主导且与提示复杂度和模型架构相关；模糊率随词汇密度、输出困惑度和输出长度增加而上升，随词汇复杂度、自一致性和回复-提示相似度增加而下降。揭示了能力-安全混淆现象（capability-safety confound），且聚合均值排行榜在模糊性普遍存在时数学上不稳定，模型排名在合理的模糊性处理下会发生显著变化。

Conclusion: LLM-centric安全基准不足以作为SLM安全评估的独立证据；需要针对SLM设计专门的安全评估方法，并对模糊判断的处理策略加以规范，以免产生误导性的排行榜结果。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in resource-constrained, privacy-sensitive settings, where safety and bias failures can cause security and societal risks. However, existing AI safety\slash security\slash compliance benchmarks are designed for large language models that may not transfer reliably to SLMs. We therefore ask: Can these benchmarks effectively and reliably evaluate SLMs? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale assessment of the effectiveness and robustness of these automated pipelines by evaluating five widely used benchmark suites across 26 open-source SLMs under a unified judging rubric, which assigns a score of 0, 1, or 0.5 to harmful, safe, or ambiguous/irrelevant responses, respectively. Across the benchmarks, ambiguous judgments dominate and correlate with prompt complexity and model architecture, indicating that {\em LLM-centric safety benchmarks are insufficient as standalone evidence for SLM safety assessment}. In general, the ambiguity rate increases with lexical density, output perplexity, and output length and decreases with lexical sophistication, self-coherence, and reply-prompt similarity. This reveals a capability-safety confound that mixes model capability with apparent safety. Since ambiguity is prevalent, aggregate mean-score leaderboards are mathematically brittle: model rankings change significantly under reasonable ambiguity treatments, even when the underlying outputs remain unchanged.

</details>


### [103] [Fool's Gold: Defensive Deception Against Safety-Removal Attacks on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17202)
*Mark Russinovich*

Main category: cs.AI
